# Runtime Fixture Canonicalization

Implemented caller-owned Space document preparation and export input, test-only bundled inputs and actor vectors, empty Rewriting startup, native label/refusal policy ownership, and board snapshot APIs with neutral normal/ported snapshot identifiers. No compatibility aliases remain.

## Planner Testing Envelope Removal

Removed `WorkflowFixture` from production Rust/TypeScript and deleted its host ABI operations 1537/1538 and WFP1 retained structural cursor. Real `WorkflowSnapshot` continues to own canonical DSL/pack. Five former fixture artifact pairs are plain JSON testing examples, replayed by Rust and TypeScript; TypeScript independently checks graphology reachability, Rust independently checks serde_json and canonical domain DSL/pack equivalence. Updated production host schema describes only media filtering and format normalization. All bounded/cancellable service laws remain for those actual operations.

## Authored Learning Content

Playbook procedural's hex-column data is the learning module's actual instructional program (runtime consumers render the exercise, generate its parameter controls, and export its solid geometry). It has no test file dependency. The owner and API now call this a learning program rather than a fixture; the prior comment describing a copied fixture workaround was removed. Jack curated graph assets similarly have graph-example catalogue contracts and labels. These are intentional authored product content rather than test witnesses.

## Graph Query and Wires Snapshot Contracts

Graph query result JSON, Wires persisted identity snapshots, Puzzle composed board snapshots and all consumers now name actual domain state. Wires identity schema is canonical `reasoning.wires.identity.snapshot`; the graph/layout schema remains the canonical neutral board schema. ## Board Snapshot and Development Server Vocabulary

Remaining board helper APIs, domain schema identifiers, and actual development-server handles have domain names. Puzzle2d import admits its one canonical payload field (JSON text or object), with obsolete json/fixture input aliases removed.

## Engine Scene and Working Graph Ownership

Real 3D engine scene snapshots and object models have precise domain names; SceneConfig carries sceneSnapshot. Rewrite mutations edit the actual working graph and use the canonical edit-working-graph keyword, updated across codecs and shared witnesses. Binary media documents retain their format bytes.

The actual discovered inventory document contract is authored at `📦️artifacts/🧭️package-inventory/🧬️schema/🔣️.json`, which describes inventory payloads rather than accepted/rejected example envelopes. Added payload-level language-neutral Ajv/jsonschema verdict test at `🧪️tests/📦️artifact-packages/🧬️schema/🟦️.ts`.

Native recheck execution: ticket-private fixed Nx RUN targets dispatch relative owner script + arguments through actual cache NativeScript, preserving required selected Cargo owner preparation. Registered four portable launch/seed profiles. The first generic --args dispatch failed from nested shell quote corruption before execution; fixed target commands remove that quoting seam. Four final rechecks now record `runtime-{trinity,flow,puzzle2d,puzzle5d}-isolated-owner-final.log`; outcomes pending. No direct Cargo bypass was introduced.
